device=\"cpu\",\n   262→                                            pin_memory=self.pin_memory)\n   263→        self.slot_mapping_np = self.slot_mapping_cpu.numpy()\n   264→        self.query_start_loc_cpu = torch.empty(self.max_num_reqs + 1,\n   265→                                               dtype=torch.int32,\n   266→                                               device=\"cpu\",\n   267→                                               pin_memory=self.pin_memory)\n   268→        self.query_start_loc_np = self.query_start_loc_cpu.numpy()\n   269→        self.seq_lens_cpu = torch.empty(self.max_num_reqs,\n   270→                                        dtype=torch.int32,\n   271→                                        device=\"cpu\",\n   272→                                        pin_memory=self.pin_memory)\n   273→        self.seq_lens_np = self.seq_lens_cpu.numpy()\n   274→\n   275→    def _update_states(self, scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\") -> None:\n   276→        \"\"\"Update the cached states and the persistent batch with the scheduler\n   277→        output.\n   278→\n   279→        The updated states are used by the `_prepare_inputs` function to create\n   280→        the input GPU tensors for the model.\n   281→\n   282→        The SamplingMetadata is updated and copied to the GPU if there is a\n   283→        new/resumed/paused/finished request in the batch.\n   284→        \"\"\"\n   285→        # Remove finished requests from the cached states.\n   286→        for req_id in scheduler_output.finished_req_ids:\n   287→            self.requests.pop(req_id, None)\n   288→            self.encoder_cache.pop(req_id, None)\n   289→        # Remove the finished requests from the persistent batch.\n   290→        # NOTE(woosuk): There could be an edge case where finished_req_ids and\n   291→        # scheduled_req_ids overlap. This happens when a request is aborted and\n   292→        # then resubmitted with the same ID. In this case, we treat them as two\n   293→        # distinct requests - clearing the cached states for the first request\n   294→        # and handling the second as a new request.\n   295→        removed_req_indices: list[int] = []\n   296→        for req_id in scheduler_output.finished_req_ids:\n   297→            req_index = self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)\n   298→            if req_index is not None:\n   299→                removed_req_indices.append(req_index)\n   300→\n   301→        # Free the cached encoder outputs.\n   302→        for req_id, input_id in scheduler_output.free_encoder_input_ids:\n   303→            encoder_outputs = self.encoder_cache.get(req_id)\n   304→            if encoder_outputs is not None:\n   305→                encoder_outputs.pop(input_id, None)\n   306→                if not encoder_outputs:\n   307→                    self.encoder_cache.pop(req_id, None)\n   308→\n   309→        # Remove the unscheduled requests from the persistent batch.\n   310→        # NOTE(woosuk): The unscheduled requests are either preempted requests\n   311→        # or running requests that are not scheduled in this step. We remove\n   312→        # them from the persistent batch but keep their cached states since\n   313→        # they will be scheduled again sometime in the future.\n   314→        scheduled_req_ids = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens.keys()\n   315→        cached_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.keys()\n   316→        unscheduled_req_ids = cached_req_ids - scheduled_req_ids\n   317→        # NOTE(woosuk): The persistent batch optimization assumes that\n   318→        # consecutive batches contain mostly the same requests. If batches\n   319→        # have low request overlap (e.g., alternating between two distinct\n   320→        # sets of requests), this optimization becomes very inefficient.\n   321→        for req_id in unscheduled_req_ids:\n   322→            req_index = self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)\n   323→            assert req_index is not None\n   324→            removed_req_indices.append(req_index)\n   325→\n   326→        req_ids_to_add: list[str] = []\n   327→        # Add new requests to the cached states.\n   328→        for new_req_data in scheduler_output.scheduled_new_reqs:\n   329→            req_id = new_req_data.req_id\n   330→            sampling_params = new_req_data.sampling_params\n   331→            if sampling_params.sampling_type == SamplingType.RANDOM_SEED:\n   332→                generator = torch.Generator(device=self.device)\n   333→                generator.manual_seed(sampling_params.seed)\n   334→            else:\n   335→                generator = None\n   336→\n   337→            self.requests[req_id] = CachedRequestState(\n   338→                req_id=req_id,\n   339→                prompt_token_ids=new_req_data.prompt_token_ids,\n   340→                prompt=new_req_data.prompt,\n   341→                mm_inputs=new_req_data.mm_inputs,\n   342→                mm_positions=new_req_data.mm_positions,\n   343→                sampling_params=sampling_params,\n   344→                generator=generator,\n   345→                block_ids=new_req_data.block_ids,\n   346→                num_computed_tokens=new_req_data.num_computed_tokens,\n   347→                output_token_ids=[],\n   348→                lora_request=new_req_data.lora_request,\n   349→            )\n   350→\n   351→            # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)\n   352→            if self.uses_mrope:\n   353→                image_grid_thw = []\n   354→                video_grid_thw = []\n   355→                second_per_grid_ts = []\n   356→                for mm_input in self.requests[req_id].mm_inputs:\n   357→                    if mm_input.get(\"image_grid_thw\") is not None:\n   358→                        image_grid_thw.extend(\n   359→                            mm_input[\"image_grid_thw\"].tolist())\n   360→                    if mm_input.get(\"video_grid_thw\") is not None:\n   361→                        video_grid_thw.extend(\n   362→                            mm_input[\"video_grid_thw\"].tolist())\n   363→                    if mm_input.get(\"second_per_grid_ts\") is not None:\n   364→                        second_per_grid_ts.extend(\n   365→                            mm_input[\"second_per_grid_ts\"])\n   366→\n   367→                hf_config = self.model_config.hf_config\n   368→\n   369→                self.requests[req_id].mrope_positions, \\\n   370→                    self.requests[req_id].mrope_position_delta = \\\n   371→                    MRotaryEmbedding.get_input_positions_tensor(\n   372→                        self.requests[req_id].prompt_token_ids,\n   373→                        hf_config=hf_config,\n   374→                        image_grid_thw=image_grid_thw,\n   375→                        video_grid_thw=video_grid_thw,\n   376→                        second_per_grid_ts=second_per_grid_ts,\n   377→                    )\n   378→\n   379→            req_ids_to_add.append(req_id)\n   380→\n   381→        # Update the states of the running/resumed requests.\n   382→        for req_data in scheduler_output.scheduled_cached_reqs:\n   383→            req_id = req_data.req_id\n   384→            req_state = self.requests[req_id]\n   385→\n   386→            # Update the cached states.\n   387→            num_computed_tokens = req_data.num_computed_tokens\n   388→            req_state.num_computed_tokens = num_computed_tokens\n   389→            # Add the sampled token(s) from the previous step (if any).\n   390→            # This doesn't include \"unverified\" tokens like spec decode tokens.\n   391→            num_new_tokens = (num_computed_tokens +\n   392→                              len(req_data.new_token_ids) -\n   393→                              req_state.num_tokens)\n   394→            if num_new_tokens == 1:\n   395→                # Avoid slicing list in most common case.\n   396→                req_state.output_token_ids.append(req_data.new_token_ids[-1])\n   397→            elif num_new_tokens > 0:\n   398→                req_state.output_token_ids.extend(\n   399→                    req_data.new_token_ids[-num_new_tokens:])\n   400→            # Update the block IDs.\n   401→            if not req_data.resumed_from_preemption:\n   402→                # Append the new blocks to the existing block IDs.\n   403→                req_state.block_ids.extend(req_data.new_block_ids)\n   404→            else:\n   405→                # The request is resumed from preemption.\n   406→                # Replace the existing block IDs with the new ones.\n   407→                req_state.block_ids = req_data.new_block_ids\n   408→\n   409→            req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.get(req_id)\n   410→            if req_index is None:\n   411→                # The request is not in the persistent batch.\n   412→                # The request was either preempted and resumed later, or was not\n   413→                # scheduled in the previous step and needs to be added again.\n   414→                req_ids_to_add.append(req_id)\n   415→                continue\n   416→\n   417→            # Update the persistent batch.\n   418→            self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_index] = (\n   419→                num_computed_tokens)\n   420→            self.input_batch.block_table.append_row(req_data.new_block_ids,\n   421→                                                    req_index)\n   422→            # Add new_token_ids to token_ids_cpu.\n   423→            start_token_index = num_computed_tokens\n   424→            end_token_index = num_computed_tokens + len(req_data.new_token_ids)\n   425→            self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[\n   426→                req_index,\n   427→                start_token_index:end_token_index] = req_data.new_token_ids\n   428→            self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] = end_token_index\n   429→            # Add spec_token_ids to token_ids_cpu.\n   430→            spec_token_ids = scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens.get(\n   431→                req_id, ())\n   432→            if spec_token_ids:\n   433→                start_index = end_token_index\n   434→                end_token_index += len(spec_token_ids)\n   435→                self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[\n   436→                    req_index, start_index:end_token_index] = spec_token_ids\n   437→            # NOTE(woosuk): `num_tokens` here may include spec decode tokens.\n   438→            self.input_batch.num_tokens[req_index] = end_token_index\n   439→\n   440→        # Check if the batch has changed. If not, we can skip copying the\n   441→        # sampling metadata from CPU to GPU.\n   442→        batch_changed = len(removed_req_indices) > 0 or len(req_ids_to_add) > 0\n   443→\n   444→        # Add the new or resumed requests to the persistent batch.\n   445→        # The smaller empty indices are filled first.\n   446→        removed_req_indices = sorted(removed_req_indices, reverse=True)\n   447→        for req_id in req_ids_to_add:\n   448→            req_state = self.requests[req_id]\n   449→            if removed_req_indices:\n   450→                # Fill the empty index.\n   451→                req_index = removed_req_indices.pop()\n   452→            else:\n   453→                # Append to the end.\n   454→                req_index = None\n   455→            self.input_batch.add_request(req_state, req_index)\n   456→\n   457→        # Condense the batched states if there are empty indices.\n   458→        if removed_req_indices:\n   459→            self.input_batch.condense(removed_req_indices)\n   460→\n   461→        if batch_changed:\n   462→            self.input_batch.refresh_sampling_metadata()\n   463→\n   464→    def _prepare_inputs(\n   465→        self,\n   466→        scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\",\n   467→    ) -> tuple[FlashAttentionMetadata, torch.Tensor,\n   468→               Optional[SpecDecodeMetadata]]:\n   469→        total_num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens\n   470→        assert total_num_scheduled_tokens > 0\n   471→        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs\n   472→        assert num_reqs > 0\n   473→\n   474→        # Some attention backends (namely MLA) may want to separate requests\n   475→        # based on if the attention computation will be compute-bound or\n   476→        # memory-bound. This gives them a hook to do that.\n   477→        modified_batch = self.attn_metadata_builder.reorder_batch(\n   478→            self.input_batch, scheduler_output)\n   479→        if modified_batch:\n   480→            self.input_batch.refresh_sampling_metadata()\n   481→\n   482→        # OPTIMIZATION: Start copying the block table first.\n   483→        # This way, we can overlap the copy with the following CPU operations.\n   484→        self.input_batch.block_table.commit(num_reqs)\n   485→\n   486→        # Get the number of scheduled tokens for each request.\n   487→        req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids\n   488→        tokens = [scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[i] for i in req_ids]\n   489→        num_scheduled_tokens = np.array(tokens, dtype=np.int32)\n   490→        max_num_scheduled_tokens = int(max(tokens))\n   491→\n   492→        # Get request indices.\n   493→        # E.g., [2, 5, 3] -> [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 2]\n   494→        req_indices = np.repeat(self.arange_np[:num_reqs],\n   495→                                num_scheduled_tokens)\n   496→\n   497→        # Get batched arange.\n   498→        # E.g., [2, 5, 3] -> [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]\n   499→        # Equivalent to but faster than:\n   500→        # np.concatenate([np.arange(n) for n in num_scheduled_tokens])\n   501→        # Step 1. [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10]\n   502→        cu_num_tokens = np.cumsum(num_scheduled_tokens)\n   503→        # Step 2. [2, 7, 10] -> [0, 0, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 7, 7, 7]\n   504→        cumsums_offsets = np.repeat(cu_num_tokens - num_scheduled_tokens,\n   505→                                    num_scheduled_tokens)\n   506→        # Step 3. [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]\n   507→        arange = self.arange_np[:total_num_scheduled_tokens] - cumsums_offsets\n   508→\n   509→        # Get positions.\n   510→        positions_np = self.positions_np[:total_num_scheduled_tokens]\n   511→        np.add(self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_indices],\n   512→               arange,\n   513→               out=positions_np)\n   514→\n   515→        # Calculate M-RoPE positions.\n   516→        # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)\n   517→        if self.uses_mrope:\n   518→            self._calc_mrope_positions(scheduler_output)\n   519→\n   520→        # Get token indices.\n   521→        # E.g., [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]\n   522→        # -> [0, 1, M, M + 1, M + 2, M + 3, M + 4, 2 * M, 2 * M + 1, 2 * M + 2]\n   523→        # where M is the max_model_len.\n   524→        token_indices = (positions_np +\n   525→                         req_indices * self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu.shape[1])\n   526→\n   527→        # NOTE(woosuk): We use torch.index_select instead of np.take here\n   528→        # because torch.index_select is much faster than np.take for large\n   529→        # tensors.\n   530→        torch.index_select(self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu_tensor.flatten(),\n   531→                           0,\n   532→                           torch.from_numpy(token_indices),\n   533→                           out=self.input_ids_cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens])\n   534→\n   535→        # Calculate the slot mapping.\n   536→        # E.g., [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]\n   537→        # -> [0, 0, K, K, K + 1, K + 1, K + 2, 2 * K, 2 * K, 2 * K + 1]\n   538→        # where K is the max_num_blocks_per_req and the block size is 2.\n   539→        # NOTE(woosuk): We can't simply use `token_indices // block_size` here\n   540→        # because M (max_model_len) is not necessarily divisible by block_size.\n   541→        block_table_indices = (req_indices * self.max_num_blocks_per_req +\n   542→                               positions_np // self.block_size)\n   543→        # NOTE(woosuk): We use torch.index_select instead of np.take here\n   544→        # because torch.index_select is much faster than np.take for large\n   545→        # tensors.\n   546→        block_table_cpu = self.input_batch.block_table.get_cpu_tensor()\n   547→        block_numbers = block_table_cpu.flatten()[block_table_indices].numpy()\n   548→        block_offsets = positions_np % self.block_size\n   549→        np.add(block_numbers * self.block_size,\n   550→               block_offsets,\n   551→               out=self.slot_mapping_np[:total_num_scheduled_tokens])\n   552→\n   553→        # Prepare the attention metadata.\n   554→        self.query_start_loc_np[0] = 0\n   555→        self.query_start_loc_np[1:num_reqs + 1] = cu_num_tokens\n   556→\n   557→        self.seq_lens_np[:num_reqs] = (\n   558→            self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs] +\n   559→            num_scheduled_tokens)\n   560→\n   561→        # Copy the tensors to the GPU.\n   562→        self.input_ids[:total_num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(\n   563→            self.input_ids_cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens], non_blocking=True)\n   564→        if self.uses_mrope:\n   565→            # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)\n   566→            self.mrope_positions[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(\n   567→                self.mrope_positions_cpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens],\n   568→                non_blocking=True)\n   569→        else:\n   570→            # Common case (1D positions)\n   571→            self.positions[:total_num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(\n   572→                self.positions_cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],\n   573→                non_blocking=True)\n   574→\n   575→        # Prepare for cascade attention if enabled & beneficial.\n   576→        common_prefix_len = 0\n   577→        if self.cascade_attn_enabled:\n   578→            common_prefix_len = self._compute_cascade_attn_prefix_len(\n   579→                num_scheduled_tokens,\n   580→                scheduler_output.num_common_prefix_blocks,\n   581→            )\n   582→\n   583→        attn_metadata = self.attn_metadata_builder.build(\n   584→            num_reqs=num_reqs,\n   585→            num_actual_tokens=total_num_scheduled_tokens,\n   586→            max_query_len=max_num_scheduled_tokens,\n   587→            common_prefix_len=common_prefix_len,\n   588→        )\n   589→\n   590→        use_spec_decode = len(\n   591→            scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens) > 0\n   592→        if not use_spec_decode:\n   593→            # NOTE(woosuk): Due to chunked prefills, the batch may contain\n   594→            # partial requests. While we should not sample any token\n   595→            # from these partial requests, we do so for simplicity.\n   596→            # We will ignore the sampled tokens from the partial requests.\n   597→            # TODO: Support prompt logprobs.\n   598→            logits_indices = attn_metadata.query_start_loc[1:] - 1\n   599→            spec_decode_metadata = None\n   600→        else:\n   601→            # Get the number of draft tokens for each request.\n   602→            # Iterate over the dictionary rather than all requests since not all\n   603→            # requests have draft tokens.\n   604→            num_draft_tokens = np.zeros(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)\n   605→            for req_id, draft_token_ids in (\n   606→                    scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens.items()):\n   607→                req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]\n   608→                num_draft_tokens[req_idx] = len(draft_token_ids)\n   609→\n   610→            spec_decode_metadata = self._calc_spec_decode_metadata(\n   611→                num_draft_tokens, cu_num_tokens)\n   612→            logits_indices = spec_decode_metadata.logits_indices\n   613→\n   614→        # Hot-Swap lora model\n   615→        if self.lora_config:\n   616→            self.set_active_loras(self.input_batch, num_scheduled_tokens)\n   617→\n   618→        return attn_metadata, logits_indices, spec_decode_metadata\n   619→\n   620→    def _compute_cascade_attn_prefix_len(\n   621→        self,\n   622→        num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,\n   623→        num_common_prefix_blocks: int,\n   624→    ) -> int:\n   625→        \"\"\"Compute the length of the common prefix for cascade attention.\n   626→\n   627→        NOTE(woosuk): The common prefix length returned by this function\n   628→        represents the length used specifically for cascade attention, not the\n   629→        actual number of tokens shared between requests. When cascade attention\n   630→        is disabled (use_cascade=False), this function returns 0 even if\n   631→        requests share common tokens. Additionally, the common prefix length is\n   632→        truncated to a multiple of the block size and may be further truncated\n   633→        due to implementation details explained below.\n   634→\n   635→        Args:\n   636→            num_scheduled_tokens: Number of tokens scheduled per request.\n   637→            num_common_prefix_blocks: Number of shared KV cache blocks.\n   638→\n   639→        Returns:\n   640→            int: Length of common prefix in tokens.\n   641→        \"\"\"\n   642→        common_prefix_len = num_common_prefix_blocks * self.block_size\n   643→        if common_prefix_len == 0:\n   644→            # Common case.\n   645→            return 0\n   646→\n   647→        # NOTE(woosuk): Cascade attention uses two attention kernels: one\n   648→        # for the common prefix and the other for the rest. For the first\n   649→        # kernel, we concatenate all the query tokens (possibly from\n   650→        # different requests) and treat them as if they are from the same\n   651→        # request. Then, we use bi-directional attention to process the\n   652→        # common prefix in the KV cache. Importantly, this means that the\n   653→        # first kernel does not do any masking.\n   654→\n   655→        # Consider the following example:\n   656→        # Request 1's input query: [D, E, X]\n   657→        # Request 1's kv cache: [A, B, C, D, E, X]\n   658→        # Request 1's num_computed_tokens: 3 (i.e., [A, B, C])\n   659→        # Request 2's input query: [E, Y]\n   660→        # Request 2's kv cache: [A, B, C, D, E, Y]\n   661→        # Request 2's num_computed_tokens: 4 (i.e., [A, B, C, D])\n   662→\n   663→        # If we use [A, B, C, D, E] as the common prefix, then the\n   664→        # first kernel will compute the bi-directional attention between\n   665→        # input query [D, E, X, E, Y] and common prefix [A, B, C, D, E].\n   666→        # However, this is wrong because D in Request 1 should not attend to\n   667→        # E in the common prefix (i.e., we need masking).\n   668→        # To avoid this, [A, B, C, D] should be the common prefix.\n   669→        # That is, the common prefix should be capped by the minimum\n   670→        # num_computed_tokens among the requests, and plus one to include\n   671→        # the first token of the query.\n   672→\n   673→        # In practice, we use [A, B, C] as the common prefix, instead of\n   674→        # [A, B, C, D] (i.e., the common prefix is capped by the minimum\n   675→        # num_computed_tokens, without plus one).\n   676→        # This is because of an implementation detail: We want to always\n   677→        # use two kernels for cascade attention. Let's imagine:\n   678→        # Request 3's input query: [D]\n   679→        # Request 3's kv cache: [A, B, C, D]\n   680→        # Request 3's num_computed_tokens: 3 (i.e., [A, B, C])\n   681→        # If we use [A, B, C, D] as the common prefix for Request 1-3,\n   682→        # then Request 3 will be processed only by the first kernel,\n   683→        # and the second kernel will get an empty input. While this is not\n   684→        # a fundamental problem, our current implementation does not support\n   685→        # this case.\n   686→        num_reqs = len(num_scheduled_tokens)\n   687→        common_prefix_len = min(\n   688→            common_prefix_len,\n   689→            self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs].min())\n   690→        # common_prefix_len should be a multiple of the block size.\n   691→        common_prefix_len = (common_prefix_len // self.block_size *\n   692→                             self.block_size)\n   693→        use_cascade = self.attn_backend.use_cascade_attention(\n   694→            common_prefix_len=common_prefix_len,\n   695→            query_lens=num_scheduled_tokens,\n   696→            num_query_heads=self.num_query_heads,\n   697→            num_kv_heads=self.num_kv_heads,\n   698→            use_alibi=self.use_alibi,\n   699→            use_sliding_window=self.window_size is not None,\n   700→            num_sms=self.num_sms,\n   701→        )\n   702→        return common_prefix_len if use_cascade else 0\n   703→\n   704→    def _calc_mrope_positions(self, scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\"):\n   705→        mrope_pos_ptr = 0\n   706→        for index, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):\n   707→            req = self.requests[req_id]\n   708→            assert req.mrope_positions is not None\n   709→\n   710→            num_computed_tokens = \\\n   711→                self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[index]\n   712→            num_scheduled_tokens = \\\n   713→                scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]\n   714→            num_prompt_tokens = len(req.prompt_token_ids)\n   715→\n   716→            if num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens > num_prompt_tokens:\n   717→                prompt_part_len = max(0,\n   718→                                      num_prompt_tokens - num_computed_tokens)\n   719→                completion_part_len = max(\n   720→                    0, num_scheduled_tokens - prompt_part_len)\n   721→            else:\n   722→                prompt_part_len = num_scheduled_tokens\n   723→                completion_part_len = 0\n   724→\n   725→            assert num_scheduled_tokens == prompt_part_len + completion_part_len\n   726→\n   727→            if prompt_part_len > 0:\n   728→                # prompt's mrope_positions are pre-computed\n   729→                dst_start = mrope_pos_ptr\n   730→                dst_end = mrope_pos_ptr + prompt_part_len\n   731→                src_start = num_computed_tokens\n   732→                src_end = num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len\n   733→\n   734→                self.mrope_positions_cpu[:, dst_start:dst_end] = \\\n   735→                    req.mrope_positions[:,src_start:src_end]\n   736→\n   737→                mrope_pos_ptr += prompt_part_len\n   738→\n   739→            if completion_part_len > 0:\n   740→                # compute completion's mrope_positions on-the-fly\n   741→                dst_start = mrope_pos_ptr\n   742→                dst_end = mrope_pos_ptr + completion_part_len\n   743→\n   744→                self.mrope_positions_cpu[:, dst_start:dst_end] = \\\n   745→                    MRotaryEmbedding.get_next_input_positions_tensor(\n   746→                        req.mrope_position_delta,\n   747→                        context_len=num_computed_tokens +\n   748→                        prompt_part_len,\n   749→                        seq_len=num_computed_tokens +\n   750→                        prompt_part_len +\n   751→                        completion_part_len,\n   752→                    )\n   753→\n   754→                mrope_pos_ptr += completion_part_len\n   755→\n   756→    def _calc_spec_decode_metadata(\n   757→        self,\n   758→        num_draft_tokens: np.ndarray,\n   759→        cu_num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,\n   760→    ) -> SpecDecodeMetadata:\n   761→        # Inputs:\n   762→        # cu_num_scheduled_tokens:  [  4, 104, 107, 207, 209]\n   763→        # num_draft_tokens:         [  3,   0,   2,   0,   1]\n   764→        # Outputs:\n   765→        # cu_num_draft_tokens:      [  3,   3,   5,   5,   6]\n   766→        # logits_indices:           [  0,   1,   2,   3, 103, 104, 105, 106,\n   767→        #                            206, 207, 208]\n   768→        # target_logits_indices:    [  0,   1,   2,   5,   6,   9]\n   769→        # bonus_logits_indices:     [  3,   4,   7,   8,  10]\n   770→\n   771→        # Compute the logits indices.\n   772→        # [4, 1, 3, 1, 2]\n   773→        num_sampled_tokens = num_draft_tokens + 1\n   774→        # Step 1. [4, 5, 8, 9, 11]\n   775→        cu_num_sampled_tokens = np.cumsum(num_sampled_tokens, dtype=np.int32)\n   776→        total_num_sampled_tokens = cu_num_sampled_tokens[-1]\n   777→        # Step 2. [0, 0, 0, 0, 4, 5, 5, 5, 8, 9, 9]\n   778→        cumsums_offsets = np.repeat(cu_num_sampled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens,\n   779→                                    num_sampled_tokens)\n   780→        # Step 3. [0, 1, 2, 3, 0, 0, 1, 2, 0, 0, 1]\n   781→        arange = self.arange_np[:total_num_sampled_tokens] - cumsums_offsets\n   782→        # Step 4. [0, 0, 0, 0, 103, 104, 104, 104, 206, 207, 207]\n   783→        logits_indices = np.repeat(\n   784→            cu_num_scheduled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens, num_sampled_tokens)\n   785→        # Step 5. [0, 1, 2, 3, 103, 104, 105, 106, 206, 207, 208]\n   786→        logits_indices += arange\n   787→\n   788→        # Compute the bonus logits indices.\n   789→        bonus_logits_indices = cu_num_sampled_tokens - 1\n   790→\n   791→        # Compute the draft logits indices.\n   792→        # [3, 3, 5, 5, 6]\n   793→        cu_num_draft_tokens = np.cumsum(num_draft_tokens, dtype=np.int32)\n   794→        total_num_draft_tokens = cu_num_draft_tokens[-1]\n   795→        # [0, 0, 0, 3, 3, 5]\n   796→        cumsums_offsets = np.repeat(cu_num_draft_tokens - num_draft_tokens,\n   797→                                    num_draft_tokens)\n   798→        # [0, 1, 2, 0, 1, 0]\n   799→        arange = self.arange_np[:total_num_draft_tokens] - cumsums_offsets\n   800→        # [0, 0, 0, 5, 5, 9]\n   801→        target_logits_indices = np.repeat(\n   802→            cu_num_sampled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens, num_draft_tokens)\n   803→        # [0, 1, 2, 5, 6, 9]\n   804→        target_logits_indices += arange\n   805→\n   806→        # TODO: Optimize the CPU -> GPU copy.\n   807→        cu_num_draft_tokens = torch.from_numpy(cu_num_draft_tokens).to(\n   808→            self.device, non_blocking=True)\n   809→        logits_indices = torch.from_numpy(logits_indices).to(self.device,\n   810→                                                             non_blocking=True)\n   811→        target_logits_indices = torch.from_numpy(target_logits_indices).to(\n   812→            self.device, non_blocking=True)\n   813→        bonus_logits_indices = torch.from_numpy(bonus_logits_indices).to(\n   814→            self.device, non_blocking=True)\n   815→\n   816→        # Compute the draft token ids.\n   817→        # draft_token_indices:      [  1,   2,   3, 105, 106, 208]\n   818→        draft_token_ids = self.input_ids[logits_indices]\n   819→        draft_token_ids = draft_token_ids[target_logits_indices + 1]\n   820→\n   821→        metadata = SpecDecodeMetadata(\n   822→            draft_token_ids=draft_token_ids,\n   823→            num_draft_tokens=num_draft_tokens.tolist(),\n   824→            cu_num_draft_tokens=cu_num_draft_tokens,\n   825→            target_logits_indices=target_logits_indices,\n   826→            bonus_logits_indices=bonus_logits_indices,\n   827→            logits_indices=logits_indices,\n   828→        )\n   829→        return metadata\n   830→\n   831→    def _execute_mm_encoder(self, scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\"):\n   832→        scheduled_encoder_inputs = scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs\n   833→        if not scheduled_encoder_inputs:\n   834→            return\n   835→\n   836→        # Batch the multi-modal inputs.\n   837→        mm_inputs = list[MultiModalKwargs]()\n   838→        req_ids_pos = list[tuple[str, int, PlaceholderRange]]()\n   839→        for req_id, encoder_input_ids in scheduled_encoder_inputs.items():\n   840→            req_state = self.requests[req_id]\n   841→\n   842→            for mm_input_id in encoder_input_ids:\n   843→                mm_inputs.append(req_state.mm_inputs[mm_input_id])\n   844→                req_ids_pos.append(\n   845→                    (req_id, mm_input_id, req_state.mm_positions[mm_input_id]))\n   846→\n   847→        # Batch mm inputs as much as we can: if a request in the batch has\n   848→        # multiple modalities or a different modality than the previous one,\n   849→        # we process it separately to preserve item order.\n   850→        # FIXME(ywang96): This is a hacky way to deal with multiple modalities\n   851→        # in the same batch while still being able to benefit from batching\n   852→        # multimodal inputs. The proper solution should be reordering the\n   853→        # encoder outputs.\n   854→        grouped_mm_inputs_list = group_mm_inputs_by_modality(mm_inputs)\n   855→\n   856→        encoder_outputs = []\n   857→        for grouped_mm_inputs in grouped_mm_inputs_list:\n   858→            batched_mm_inputs = MultiModalKwargs.batch(grouped_mm_inputs)\n   859→            batched_mm_inputs = MultiModalKwargs.as_kwargs(batched_mm_inputs,\n   860→                                                           device=self.device)\n   861→\n   862→            # Run the encoder.\n   863→            # `curr_group_outputs` is either of the following:\n   864→            # 1. A tensor of shape (num_items, feature_size, hidden_size)\n   865→            # in case feature_size is fixed across all multimodal items.\n   866→            # 2. A list or tuple (length: num_items) of tensors, each of shape\n   867→            # (feature_size, hidden_size) in case the feature size is dynamic\n   868→            # depending on the input multimodal items.\n   869→            curr_group_outputs = self.model.get_multimodal_embeddings(\n   870→                **batched_mm_inputs)\n   871→\n   872→            sanity_check_mm_encoder_outputs(\n   873→                curr_group_outputs,\n   874→                expected_num_items=len(grouped_mm_inputs),\n   875→            )\n   876→\n   877→            for output in curr_group_outputs:\n   878→                encoder_outputs.append(output)\n   879→\n   880→        # Cache the encoder outputs.\n   881→        for (req_id, input_id, pos_info), output in zip(\n   882→                req_ids_pos,\n   883→                encoder_outputs,\n   884→        ):\n   885→            if req_id not in self.encoder_cache:\n   886→                self.encoder_cache[req_id] = {}\n   887→\n   888→            self.encoder_cache[req_id][input_id] = scatter_mm_placeholders(\n   889→                output,\n   890→                is_embed=pos_info.is_embed,\n   891→            )\n   892→\n   893→    def _gather_mm_embeddings(\n   894→        self,\n   895→        scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\",\n   896→    ) -> list[torch.Tensor]:\n   897→        mm_embeds: list[torch.Tensor] = []\n   898→        for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:\n   899→            num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[\n   900→                req_id]\n   901→            req_state = self.requests[req_id]\n   902→            num_computed_tokens = req_state.num_computed_tokens\n   903→            mm_positions = req_state.mm_positions\n   904→            for i, pos_info in enumerate(mm_positions):\n   905→                start_pos = pos_info.offset\n   906→                num_encoder_tokens = pos_info.length\n   907→\n   908→                # The encoder output is needed if the two ranges overlap:\n   909→                # [num_computed_tokens,\n   910→                #  num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens) and\n   911→                # [start_pos, start_pos + num_encoder_tokens)\n   912→                if start_pos >= num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens:\n   913→                    # The encoder output is not needed in this step.\n   914→                    break\n   915→                if start_pos + num_encoder_tokens <= num_computed_tokens:\n   916→                    # The encoder output is already processed and stored\n   917→                    # in the decoder's KV cache.\n   918→                    continue\n   919→\n   920→                start_idx = max(num_computed_tokens - start_pos, 0)\n   921→                end_idx = min(\n   922→                    num_computed_tokens - start_pos + num_scheduled_tokens,\n   923→                    num_encoder_tokens)\n   924→                assert start_idx < end_idx\n   925→                assert req_id in self.encoder_cache\n   926→                assert i in self.encoder_cache[req_id]\n   927→                encoder_output = self.encoder_cache[req_id][i]\n   928→\n   929→                if (is_embed := pos_info.is_embed) is not None:\n   930→                    is_embed = is_embed[start_idx:end_idx]\n   931→\n   932→                mm_embeds_item = gather_mm_placeholders(\n   933→                    encoder_output[start_idx:end_idx],\n   934→                    is_embed=is_embed,\n   935→                )\n   936→                mm_embeds.append(mm_embeds_item)\n   937→        return mm_embeds\n   938→\n   939→    def get_model(self) -> nn.Module:\n   940→        return self.model\n   941→\n   942→    def apply_grammar_bitmask(\n   943→        self,\n   944→        scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\",\n   945→        logits: torch.Tensor,\n   946→    ):\n   947→        # Serialization of np.ndarray is much more efficient than a tensor,\n   948→        # so we receive it in that format.\n   949→        grammar_bitmask = scheduler_output.grammar_bitmask\n   950→        if grammar_bitmask is None:\n   951→            return\n   952→\n   953→        # We receive the structured output bitmask from the scheduler, but the\n   954→        # indices of the requests in the batch may not match the indices of\n   955→        # the bitmask since the scheduler doesn't know how the gpu runner is\n   956→        # ordering the requests in the batch. We need to sort the bitmask to\n   957→        # match the order of the requests used here.\n   958→        struct_out_req_batch_indices: dict[str, int] = {}\n   959→        indices_match = True\n   960→        for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:\n   961→            mask_index = scheduler_output.structured_output_request_ids.get(\n   962→                req_id)\n   963→            if mask_index is None:\n   964→                # not a structured output request\n   965→                continue\n   966→            batch_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]\n   967→            if batch_index != mask_index:\n   968→                indices_match = False\n   969→            struct_out_req_batch_indices[req_id] = batch_index\n   970→\n   971→        if not indices_match:\n   972→            # Sort the bitmask to match the order of the requests\n   973→            sorted_bitmask = np.zeros_like(grammar_bitmask)\n   974→            for req_id, batch_index in struct_out_req_batch_indices.items():\n   975→                orig_index = scheduler_output.structured_output_request_ids[\n   976→                    req_id]\n   977→                sorted_bitmask[batch_index] = grammar_bitmask[orig_index]\n   978→            grammar_bitmask = sorted_bitmask\n   979→\n   980→        grammar_bitmask = torch.from_numpy(grammar_bitmask)\n   981→\n   982→        # TODO: compatibility with spec decode\n   983→        xgr.apply_token_bitmask_inplace(\n   984→            logits,\n   985→            grammar_bitmask.to(self.device, non_blocking=True),\n   986→            indices=list(struct_out_req_batch_indices.values()),\n   987→        )\n   988→\n   989→    @torch.inference_mode()\n   990→    def execute_model(\n   991→        self,\n   992→        scheduler_output: \"SchedulerOutput\",\n   993→        intermediate_tensors: Optional[IntermediateTensors] = None,\n   994→    ) -> Union[ModelRunnerOutput, torch.Tensor]:\n   995→        self._update_states(scheduler_output)\n   996→        if not scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens:\n   997→            # Return empty ModelRunnerOutput if there's no work to do.\n   998→            return EMPTY_MODEL_RUNNER_OUTPUT\n   999→\n  1000→        if self.is_multimodal_model:\n  1001→            # Run the multimodal encoder if any.\n  1002→            self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)\n  1003→            mm_embeds = self._gather_mm_embeddings(scheduler_output)\n  1004→        else:\n  1005→            mm_embeds = []\n  1006→\n  1007→        # Prepare the decoder inputs.\n  1008→        attn_metadata, logits_indices, spec_decode_metadata = (\n  1009→            self._prepare_inputs(scheduler_output))\n  1010→        num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens\n  1011→        if (self.use_cuda_graph\n  1012→                and num_scheduled_tokens <= self.cudagraph_batch_sizes[-1]):\n  1013→            # Use piecewise CUDA graphs.\n  1014→            # Add padding to the batch size.\n  1015→            num_input_tokens = self.vllm_config.pad_for_cudagraph(\n  1016→                num_scheduled_tokens)\n  1017→        else:\n  1018→            # Eager mode.\n  1019→            num_input_tokens = num_scheduled_tokens\n  1020→        attn_metadata.num_input_tokens = num_input_tokens\n  1021→\n  1022→        if self.is_multimodal_model:\n  1023→            # NOTE(woosuk): To unify token ids and soft tokens (vision\n  1024→            # embeddings), we always use embeddings (rather than token ids)\n  1025→            # as input to the multimodal model, even when the input is text.\n  1026→            input_ids = self.input_ids[:num_scheduled_tokens]\n  1027→            if mm_embeds:\n  1028→                inputs_embeds = self.model.get_input_embeddings(\n  1029→                    input_ids, mm_embeds)\n  1030→            else:\n  1031→                inputs_embeds = self.model.get_input_embeddings(input_ids)\n  1032→            # TODO(woosuk): Avoid the copy. Optimize.\n  1033→            self.inputs_embeds[:num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(inputs_embeds)\n  1034→            inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds[:num_input_tokens]\n  1035→            input_ids = None\n  1036→        else:\n  1037→            # For text-only models, we use token ids as input.\n  1038→            # While it is possible to use embeddings as input just like the\n  1039→            # multimodal models, it is not desirable for performance since\n  1040→            # then the embedding layer is not included in the CUDA graph.\n  1041→            input_ids = self.input_ids[:num_input_tokens]\n  1042→            inputs_embeds = None\n  1043→        if self.uses_mrope:\n  1044→            positions = self.mrope_positions[:, :num_input_tokens]\n  1045→        else:\n  1046→            positions = self.positions[:num_input_tokens]\n  1047→\n  1048→        if get_pp_group().is_first_rank:\n  1049→            intermediate_tensors = None\n  1050→        else:\n  1051→            assert intermediate_tensors is not None\n  1052→            assert self.intermediate_tensors is not None\n  1053→            for k, v in intermediate_tensors.items():\n  1054→                self.intermediate_tensors[k][:num_input_tokens].copy_(\n  1055→                    v[:num_input_tokens], non_blocking=True)\n  1056→            intermediate_tensors = IntermediateTensors({\n  1057→                k: v[:num_input_tokens]\n  1058→                for k, v in self.intermediate_tensors.items()\n  1059→            })\n  1060→\n  1061→        # Run the decoder.\n  1062→        # Use persistent buffers for CUDA graphs.\n  1063→        with set_forward_context(attn_metadata, self.vllm_config):\n  1064→            hidden_states = self.model(\n  1065→                input_ids=input_ids,\n  1066→                positions=positions,\n  1067→                intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,\n  1068→                inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,\n  1069→            )\n  1070→        if not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:\n  1071→            # For mid-pipeline stages, return the hidden states.\n  1072→            return hidden_states\n  1073→\n  1074→        hidden_states = hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens]\n  1075→        sample_hidden_states = hidden_states[logits_indices]\n  1076→        logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states, None)\n  1077→\n  1078→        # Apply structured output bitmasks if present\n  1079→        if scheduler_output.grammar_bitmask is not None:\n  1080→            self.apply_grammar_bitmask(scheduler_output, logits)\n  1081→\n  1082→        # Sample the next token and get logprobs if needed.\n  1083→        sampling_metadata = self.input_batch.sampling_metadata\n  1084→        if spec_decode_metadata is None:\n  1085→            sampler_output = self.model.sample(\n  1086→                logits=logits,\n  1087→                sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,\n  1088→            )\n  1089→        else:\n  1090→            # When indexing with a tensor (bonus_logits_indices), PyTorch\n  1091→            # creates a new tensor with separate storage from the original\n  1092→            # logits tensor. This means any in-place operations on bonus_logits\n  1093→            # won't affect the original logits tensor.\n  1094→            bonus_logits = logits[spec_decode_metadata.bonus_logits_indices]\n  1095→            sampler_output = self.model.sample(\n  1096→                logits=bonus_logits,\n  1097→                sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,\n  1098→            )\n  1099→            bonus_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids\n  1100→\n  1101→            # Just like `bonus_logits`, `target_logits` is a new tensor with\n  1102→            # separate storage from the original `logits` tensor. Therefore,\n  1103→            # it is safe to update `target_logits` in place.\n  1104→            target_logits = logits[spec_decode_metadata.target_logits_indices]\n  1105→            output_token_ids = self.rejection_sampler(\n  1106→                spec_decode_metadata,\n  1107→                None,  # draft_probs\n  1108→                target_logits,\n  1109→                bonus_token_ids,\n  1110→                sampling_metadata,\n  1111→            )\n  1112→            sampler_output.sampled_token_ids = output_token_ids\n  1113→\n  1114→        # TODO(woosuk): The following loop can be slow since it iterates over\n  1115→        # the requests one by one. Optimize.\n  1116→        discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices = []\n  1117→        for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):\n  1118→            req_state = self.requests[req_id]\n  1119→            seq_len = (req_state.num_computed_tokens +\n  1120→                       scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id])\n  1121→            if seq_len < req_state.num_tokens:\n  1122→                # Ignore the sampled token for partial prefills.\n  1123→                # Rewind the generator state as if the token was not sampled.\n  1124→                # This relies on cuda-specific torch-internal impl details\n  1125→                generator = self.input_batch.generators.get(i)\n  1126→                if generator is not None:\n  1127→                    generator.set_offset(generator.get_offset() - 4)\n  1128→                # Record the index of the request that should not be sampled,\n  1129→                # so that we could clear the sampled tokens before returning.\n  1130→                discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices.append(i)\n  1131→\n  1132→        # NOTE: GPU -> CPU Sync happens here.\n  1133→        # Move as many CPU operations as possible before this sync point.\n  1134→        logprobs_tensors = sampler_output.logprobs_tensors\n  1135→        logprobs_lists = logprobs_tensors.tolists() \\\n  1136→            if logprobs_tensors is not None else None\n  1137→\n  1138→        # Compute prompt logprobs if needed.\n  1139→        prompt_logprobs_dict = self._get_prompt_logprobs_dict(\n  1140→            hidden_states,\n  1141→            scheduler_output,\n  1142→        )\n  1143→\n  1144→        # Get the valid generated tokens.\n  1145→        sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids\n  1146→        max_gen_len = sampled_token_ids.shape[-1]\n  1147→        if max_gen_len == 1:\n  1148→            # No spec decode tokens.\n  1149→            valid_sampled_token_ids = sampled_token_ids.tolist()\n  1150→        else:\n  1151→            # Includes spec decode tokens.\n  1152→            valid_sampled_token_ids = self.rejection_sampler.parse_output(\n  1153→                sampled_token_ids,\n  1154→                self.input_batch.vocab_size,\n  1155→            )\n  1156→        # Mask out the sampled tokens that should not be sampled.\n  1157→        for i in discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices:\n  1158→            valid_sampled_token_ids[i].clear()\n  1159→\n  1160→        if not self.use_spec_decode:\n  1161→            # Speculative decoding is not enabled.\n  1162→            spec_token_ids = None\n  1163→        elif self.speculative_config.method == \"ngram\":\n  1164→            assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposer)\n  1165→            spec_token_ids = self.generate_draft_token_ids(\n  1166→                valid_sampled_token_ids, sampling_metadata)\n  1167→        elif self.speculative_config.method == \"eagle\":\n  1168→            assert isinstance(self.drafter, EagleProposer)\n  1169→            # TODO(woosuk): Refactor the loop.\n  1170→            next_token_ids: list[int] = []\n  1171→            for i, token_ids in enumerate(valid_sampled_token_ids):\n  1172→                if token_ids:\n  1173→                    # Common case.\n  1174→                    next_token_id = token_ids[-1]\n  1175→                else:\n  1176→                    # Partial prefill (rare case).\n  1177→                    # Get the next token id from the request state.\n  1178→                    req_id = self.input_batch.req_ids[i]\n  1179→                    req_state = self.requests[req_id]\n  1180→                    seq_len = (req_state.num_computed_tokens +\n  1181→                               scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id])\n  1182→                    next_token_id = req_state.get_token_id(seq_len)\n  1183→                next_token_ids.append(next_token_id)\n  1184→            next_token_ids = torch.tensor(next_token_ids,\n  1185→                                          dtype=torch.int32,\n  1186→                                          device=self.device)\n  1187→\n  1188→            if spec_decode_metadata is None:\n  1189→                # Skip speculative decoding.\n  1254→                draft_token_ids.append([])\n  1255→                continue\n  1256→\n  1257→            # Skip requests that require top-p, top-k, etc.\n  1258→            req_id = self.input_batch.req_ids[i]\n  1259→            if not is_spec_decode_supported(req_id, self.input_batch):\n  1260→                draft_token_ids.append([])\n  1261→                continue\n  1262→\n  1263→            # Since prompt logprobs are a rare feature, prioritize simple,\n  1308→        # maintainable loop over optimal performance.\n  1309→        completed_prefill_reqs = []\n  1310→        for req_id, num_prompt_logprobs in num_prompt_logprobs_dict.items():\n  1311→\n  1312→            num_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]\n  1313→\n  1314→            # Get metadata for this request.\n  1315→            request = self.requests[req_id]\n  1316→            num_prompt_tokens = len(request.prompt_token_ids)\n  1317→            prompt_token_ids = torch.tensor(request.prompt_token_ids).to(\n  1318→                self.device, non_blocking=True)\n  1319→\n  1320→            # Set up target LogprobsTensors object.\n  1321→            logprobs_tensors = in_progress_dict.get(req_id)\n  1322→            if not logprobs_tensors:\n  1323→                # Create empty logprobs CPU tensors for the entire prompt.\n  1324→                # If chunked, we'll copy in slice by slice.\n  1325→                logprobs_tensors = LogprobsTensors.empty_cpu(\n  1326→                    num_prompt_tokens - 1, num_prompt_logprobs + 1)\n  1327→                in_progress_dict[req_id] = logprobs_tensors\n  1328→\n  1329→            # Determine number of logits to retrieve.\n  1330→            start_idx = request.num_computed_tokens\n  1331→            start_tok = start_idx + 1\n  1332→            num_remaining_tokens = num_prompt_tokens - start_tok\n  1333→            if num_tokens <= num_remaining_tokens:\n  1334→                # This is a chunk, more tokens remain.\n  1335→                # In the == case, there are no more prompt logprobs to produce\n  1336→                # but we want to defer returning them to the next step where we\n  1337→                # have new generated tokens to return.\n  1338→                num_logits = num_tokens\n  1339→            else:\n  1340→                # This is the last chunk of prompt tokens to return.\n  1341→                num_logits = num_remaining_tokens\n  1342→                completed_prefill_reqs.append(req_id)\n  1343→                prompt_logprobs_dict[req_id] = logprobs_tensors\n  1344→\n  1345→            if num_logits <= 0:\n  1346→                # This can happen for the final chunk if we prefilled exactly\n  1347→                # (num_prompt_tokens - 1) tokens for this request in the prior\n  1348→                # step. There are no more prompt logprobs to produce.\n  1349→                continue\n  1350→\n  1351→            # Get the logits corresponding to this req's prompt tokens.\n  1352→            # If this is a partial request (i.e. chunked prefill),\n  1353→            # then there is prompt logprob generated for each index.\n  1354→            req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]\n  1355→            offset = self.query_start_loc_np[req_idx].item()\n  1356→            prompt_hidden_states = hidden_states[offset:offset + num_logits]\n  1357→            logits = self.model.compute_logits(prompt_hidden_states, None)\n  1358→\n  1359→            # Get the \"target\" tokens for each index. For prompt at index i,\n  1360→            # the token at prompt index i+1 is the \"sampled\" token we want\n  1361→            # to gather the logprob for.\n  1362→            tgt_token_ids = prompt_token_ids[start_tok:start_tok + num_logits]\n  1363→\n  1364→            # Compute prompt logprobs.\n  1365→            logprobs = self.model.sampler.compute_logprobs(logits)\n  1366→            chunk_slice = slice(start_idx, start_idx + num_logits)\n  1371→            logprobs_tensors.logprob_token_ids[chunk_slice].copy_(\n  1372→                token_ids, non_blocking=True)\n  1373→            logprobs_tensors.logprobs[chunk_slice].copy_(logprobs,\n  1374→                                                         non_blocking=True)\n  1375→            logprobs_tensors.selected_token_ranks[chunk_slice].copy_(\n  1376→                ranks, non_blocking=True)\n  1377→\n  1378→        # Remove requests that have completed prefill from the batch\n  1379→        # num_prompt_logprobs_dict.\n  1380→        for req_id in completed_prefill_reqs:\n  1381→            del num_prompt_logprobs_dict[req_id]\n  1382→            del in_progress_dict[req_id]\n  1383→\n  1384→        # Must synchronize the non-blocking GPU->CPU transfers.\n  1385→        if prompt_logprobs_dict:\n  1386→            torch.cuda.synchronize()\n  1387→\n  1388→        return prompt_logprobs_dict\n  1389→\n  1390→    @torch.inference_mode()\n  1391→    def _dummy_run(\n  1392→        self,\n  1393→        num_tokens: int,\n  1394→    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n  1395→\n  1396→        # Set num_scheduled_tokens based on num_tokens and max_num_seqs\n  1397→        # for dummy run with LoRA so that the num_reqs collectively\n  1398→        # has num_tokens in total.\n  1399→        assert num_tokens <= self.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens\n  1400→        max_num_reqs = self.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs\n  1401→        num_reqs = max_num_reqs if num_tokens >= max_num_reqs else num_tokens\n  1402→        min_tokens_per_req = num_tokens // num_reqs\n  1403→        num_scheduled_tokens_list = [min_tokens_per_req] * num_reqs\n  1404→        num_scheduled_tokens_list[-1] += num_tokens % num_reqs\n  1405→        assert sum(num_scheduled_tokens_list) == num_tokens\n  1406→        assert len(num_scheduled_tokens_list) == num_reqs\n  1407→        num_scheduled_tokens = np.array(num_scheduled_tokens_list,\n  1408→                                        dtype=np.int32)\n  1409→\n  1410→        with self.maybe_dummy_run_with_lora(self.lora_config,\n  1411→                                            num_scheduled_tokens):\n  1412→            model = self.model\n  1413→            if self.is_multimodal_model:\n  1414→                input_ids = None\n  1415→                inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds[:num_tokens]\n  1416→            else:\n  1417→                input_ids = self.input_ids[:num_tokens]\n  1418→                inputs_embeds = None\n  1419→            if self.uses_mrope:\n  1420→                positions = self.mrope_positions[:, :num_tokens]\n  1421→            else:\n  1422→                positions = self.positions[:num_tokens]\n  1423→\n  1424→            if get_pp_group().is_first_rank:\n  1425→                intermediate_tensors = None\n  1426→            else:\n  1427→                if self.intermediate_tensors is None:\n  1428→                    self.intermediate_tensors = (\n  1429→               device=self.device))\n  1433→                intermediate_tensors = IntermediateTensors({\n  1434→                    k: v[:num_tokens]\n  1435→                    for k, v in self.intermediate_tensors.items()\n  1436→                })\n  1437→\n  1438→            with set_forward_context(None,\n  1439→                                     self.vllm_config,\n  1440→                                     num_tokens=num_tokens):\n  1441→                hidden_states = model(\n  1442→                    input_ids=input_ids,\n  1443→                    positions=positions,\n  1444→                    intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,\n  1445→                    inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,\n  1446→                )\n  1447→\n  1448→        logit_indices = np.cumsum(num_scheduled_tokens) - 1\n  1449→        return hidden_states[logit_indices]\n  1450→\n  1451→    @torch.inference_mode()\n  1452→    def _dummy_sampler_run(\n  1453→        self,\n  1454→        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,\n  1455→    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n  1456→\n  1457→        logits = self.model.compute_logits(hidden_states, None)\n  1458→        num_reqs = logits.size(0)\n  1459→\n        raise RuntimeError(\n  1489→                    \"CUDA out of memory occurred when warming up sampler with \"\n  1490→                    f\"{num_reqs} dummy requests. NOTE: Currently model is profiled with a single non-text\n  1531→            # modality with the max possible input tokens even when\n  1532→            # it supports multiple.\n  1533→            max_tokens_by_modality_dict = self.mm_registry \\\n  1534→                .get_max_tokens_per_item_by_nonzero_modality(self.model_config)\n  1535→            dummy_data_modality, max_tokens_per_mm_item = max(\n  1536→                max_tokens_by_modality_dict.items(), key=lambda item: item[1])\n  1537→\n  1538→            # Check how many items of this modality can be supported by\n  1539→            # the encoder budget.\n  1540→            encoder_budget = min(self.max_num_encoder_input_tokens,\n  1541→                                 self.encoder_cache_size)\n  1542→\n  1543→            max_num_mm_items_encoder_budget = cdiv(encoder_budget,\n  1544→                                                   max_tokens_per_mm_item)\n  1545→\n  1546→            # Check how many items of this modality can be supported by\n  1547→            # the decoder budget.\n  1548→            max_mm_items_per_req = self.mm_registry.get_mm_limits_per_prompt(\n  1549→                self.model_config)[dummy_data_modality]\n  1550→\n  1551→            # NOTE: We do not consider max_num_batched_tokens on purpose\n  1552→            # because the multimodal embeddings can be generated in advance\n  1553→            # and chunked prefilled.\n  1554→            max_num_mm_items_decoder_budget = self.max_num_reqs * \\\n  1555→                max_mm_items_per_req\n  1556→\n  1557→            max_num_mm_items = min(max_num_mm_items_encoder_budget,\n  1558→                                   max_num_mm_items_decoder_budget)\n  1559→\n  1560→            logger.info(\n  1561→                \"Encoder cache will be initialized with a budget of %s tokens,\"\n  1562→                \" and profiled with %s %s items of the maximum feature size.\",\n  1563→                encoder_budget, max_num_mm_items, dummy_data_modality)\n  1564→\n  1565→            # Please add \"\n  1605→                \"-O %s to use CUDA graphs.\", CompilationLevel.PIECEWISE)\n  1606→            return\n  1607→\n  1608→        start_time = time.perf_counter()\n  1609→        start_free_gpu_memory = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]\n  1610→\n  1611→        # Trigger CUDA graph capture for specific shapes.\n  1612→        # Capture the large shapes first so that the smaller shapes\n  1613→        # can reuse the memory pool allocated for the large shapes.\n  1614→        with graph_capture(device=self.device):\n  1615→            for num_tokens in reversed(self.cudagraph_batch_sizes):\n  1616→                for _ in range(self.vllm_config.compilation_config.\n  1617→                               cudagraph_num_of_warmups):\n  1618→                    self._dummy_run(num_tokens)\n  1619→                self._dummy_run(num_tokens)\n  1620→\n  1621→        end_time = time.perf_counter()\n  1622→        end_free_gpu_memory = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]\n  1623→        elapsed_time = end_time - start_time\n  1624→        cuda_graph_size = start_free_gpu_memory - end_free_gpu_memory\n  1625→        # This usually takes 5~20 seconds.\n  1626→        logger.info(\"Graph capturing finished in %.0f secs, took %.2f GiB\",\n  1627→                    elapsed_time, cuda_graph_size / (1 << 30))\n  1628→\n  1629→    def initialize_kv_cache(self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig) -> None:\n  1630→        \"\"\"\n  1631→        Initialize KV cache based on `kv_cache_config`.\n  1632→        Args:\n  1633→            kv_cache_config: Configuration for the KV cache, including the KV\n  1634→            cache size of each layer\n  1635→        \"\"\"\n  1636→        if len(kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) > 1:\n  1637→            raise NotImplementedError(\n  1638→                \"Hybrid models with more than one KV cache type are not \"\n  1639→                \"supported yet.\")\n  1640→\n  1641→        kv_caches: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}\n  1642→\n  1643→        for kv_cache_group in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:\n  1644→            kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec\n  1645→            for layer_name in kv_cache_group.layer_names:\n  1646→                tensor_config = kv_cache_config.tensors[layer_name]\n  1647→                assert tensor_config.size % kv_cache_spec.page_size_bytes == 0\n  1648→                num_blocks = tensor_config.size // kv_cache_spec.page_size_bytes\n  1649→                # `num_blocks` is the number of blocks the model runner can use.\n  1650→                # `kv_cache_config.num_blocks` is the number of blocks that\n  1651→                # KVCacheManager may allocate.\n  1652→                # Since different GPUs may have different number of layers and\n  1653→                # different memory capacities, `num_blocks` can be different on\n  1654→                # different GPUs, and `kv_cache_config.num_blocks` is set to\n  1655→                # the min of all `num_blocks`.
